Character/Finite.v is the substantial half, fully proven and axiom-free: `tau_iso_finite : FiniteCarrier G → G ≅ DD G` over finiteness-as-data (enumeration + ≈-completeness + ≈-decider), via a Type-valued pigeonhole toolkit (stdlib `In`/`NoDup` are Prop and cannot eliminate), `smul_annihilator` (no Lagrange, no well-ordering), the CHARACTER EXTENSION lemma (fuel recursion whose progress lemma `outside_count_generated` re-establishes its own bound — no cardinality anywhere; bounded-minimization coset decomposition with uniqueness by period-minimality; k-th roots computed in ℚ), separation with value 1/k forcing `tau_injective_finite`, and surjectivity by the SAME subgroup induction (`Realizes`/`VanishesOn` — every functional vanishing on H's annihilator is evaluation at a member of H), deliberately avoiding |D(G)| = |G| — though `zmod2_characters` verifies the count concretely on the ℤ/2 witness. Character/NonNatural.v is the negative half (`maclane:I.4:remark1`): the groupoid core `AbIso`, the covariant `D' f = D(f⁻¹)`, and `sigma_not_natural` — no family of isos `σ_G : G ≅ D(G)` survives even the single naturality square at the automorphism ×2 of `Z5` (2q ≈ 3q forces q ≈ 0, so σ(1) is the zero character and σ is not injective) — Mac Lane's m² ≡ 1 obstruction at the first working prime, p = 5
- **Instance/Mod.v**: Module categories R-Mod and friends (Mac Lane §I.7) — `RModObject` extends `AbObject` by a scalar action with the four module laws (0·m, r·0, and both negation compatibilities DERIVED, not fields), `RModHom` a group hom preserving the action (negation preservation cited from Ab's `ab_map_neg`), the category `RMod R` with forgetful functors to Ab and Sets, `RMod_Zero` on the trivial module, and Mac Lane's proposition both ways — `rmod_monic_iff` (kernel probe) and `rmod_epic_iff` (constructive cokernel quotient) — with `RModKernel`/`RModQuotient` literally riding Instance/Ab.v's `AbKernel`/`AbQuotient` (the only new obligations are kernel action-closure and the action descending to cosets). Right modules are `ModR R := RMod (Ring_op R)` over the transparent opposite ring (`Ring_op_additive_agrees` by eq_refl; involutivity deliberately not claimed — `rig_mul_assoc` routes through an opaque helper), justified by `bimodule_right_RMod : Bimodule R S → RModObject (Ring_op S)` where `Bimodule` carries the r(ms) ≈ (rm)s compatibility; `RMod_Preadditive` reuses Ab's hom-addition for the group part; the witness is `Ring_RMod` (every ring is a left module over itself, each law literally a rig law) with `Int_RMod` and eq_refl Examples. The `Field` class and the `Vct_F` name are #244's (delivered there as a `Definition`, not a notation), per that issue's QA correction — stated in the header, not built here
- **Instance/Ab/ModFunctor.v**: R-Mod as an additive functor category (Mac Lane §II.4 Exercise 1, p. 41) — the ring-as-one-object-Ab-category clause delivered by REUSE + one step: `DeloopRig` (Theory/Algebra/Rig.v) already carries the Preadditive structure, and `DeloopRing_AbEnriched` adds `abneg := ring_neg` (five `eq_refl` readings record that the hom-group IS R's additive group and composition IS multiplication; `rig_iff_one_object_preadditive` cited as the rig-level version). `AbFunAdd R := Sub [DeloopRig (ring_rig R), Ab]` on the `AdditiveFunctor` predicate (a FULL subcategory), and **`RMod_AbFun_equiv : AbFunAdd R ≅[Cat] RMod R`** (`≅[Cat]` IS equivalence, Functor_Setoid) — an additive functor is exactly a module structure on its image group, with THE PIVOT localized: `rm_smul_distr_l/assoc/one` come free from `cmon_map_plus`/`fmap_comp`/`fmap_id` (composition in the delooping IS `rig_mul`, the identity IS `rig_one`), and `rm_smul_distr_r` is EXACTLY `fmap_padd`, the sole `AdditiveFunctor` field, in both directions (`functor_of_module_additive`'s only content); one-object naturality collapses to `rm_map_smul` (the Rep.v/#279 sibling architecture, the additive enrichment added). Module-side round trip DEFINITIONAL (carrier and smul by `eq_refl`, iso legs `cmon_hom_id`); functor-side pointwise (the rebuilt `CMonHom` records are not convertible though their underlying maps are — the in-file note beside `AbFunAdd_functor_round` says exactly this). The subcategory is PROVED PROPER at `Int_Ring` (`AbFunAdd_proper` — the nontrivial-ring qualifier matters: everything-↦-id IS additive over the trivial ring): two multiplicative-but-not-additive functors — `unit_act_functor` (everything ↦ id, refuted 1 ≠ 2 at ℤ) and `square_functor` (n ↦ n²·(−) over ℤ, refuted 4 ≠ 2) — so the additive predicate genuinely cuts. Ring-level `EndRing` converse NOT built (listed in the file's WHAT-IS-NOT-BUILT; the rig-level biconditional stays Rig.v's). Witnesses `Ring_AbFun` + ℤ `eq_refl` computations (fmap 3 acts as ·3). Gotcha recorded in-file: `[C, D]` is level-90 and needs parens as a bare application argument (`Sub ([X, Y]) …`)
- **Instance/Ab/Graded.v**: Graded abelian groups as Ab^ℕ (Mac Lane §II.4 Exercise 3, p. 41) — `GradedAb` defined DIRECTLY (objects `nat → AbObject`, degreewise `AbHom`s, componentwise ≈) with the PiCat comparison recorded and its DIVERGENCE measured: objects/homs/equiv-relation/id/compose agree with `PiCat (fun _ : nat => Ab)` by `eq_refl` (five Examples, true by construction), while the records are provably not convertible — the `homset` field itself, `compose_respects`, the four laws and the `Equivalence` witness all reject (probed, not assumed) — so `PiCat_GradedAb_iso` is a real identity-on-data functor pair with `iso_id` round-trip components, and that leg is STRICTER than `≅[Cat]` (which IS equivalence, Cat's hom-setoid being `Functor_Setoid`): identity on data both ways, while the `Fun_Discrete_PiCat` leg is an equivalence proper. **`Graded_Fun_equiv : ([DiscreteCat nat, Ab]) ≅[Cat] GradedAb`** := that comparison composed with #276's `Fun_Discrete_PiCat` — REUSE, not re-proof: no transformation over the discrete shape is rebuilt. `GradedAb_shift` (13 lines) earns its keep through **`graded_degree_one`**: `(∀ n, F n ~> G (S n)) = (F ~{GradedAb}~> shift G)` by `eq_refl` — a degree-one map IS an ordinary arrow into the shift. Witnesses genuinely degree-dependent and PROVED so (`graded_deg_mul`: multiplication by n in degree n, with `graded_deg_mul_not_constant` refuting any constant-family presentation; `conc0` ℤ-concentrated-in-degree-0 with shift computations). Header disambiguates Monad/Graded.v (monoid-graded MONADS — grades interact via `T m ◯ T n ⟹ T (m·n)`; these degrees never interact) and scopes the index honestly: the DONORS are index-generic, this file's own definitions hardcode ℕ as the exercise asks — a ℤ-graded variant would reuse the donors unchanged but restate these definitions. All 44 constants Closed (25 glob + 19 Program obligations, Print Module methodology)
- **Instance/Mod/Free.v**: Mac Lane §III.1 construction 1 + §IV.1 construction 1 + Riehl §4.0 — the free module on a setoid, its universal arrow, and the free ⊣ forgetful ADJUNCTION, with `Instance/Vect/Free.v` the vector-space reading. **The issue's own sketch could not be followed and the header argues why, with the obstruction located exactly**: it asks for finitely supported K-valued functions, and the CARRIER is not the problem — a `≈`-respecting function paired with a list witnessing its support is writable over a bare setoid with no decider. The BASIS INSERTION is: the basis vector at `x` is "1 at x, 0 elsewhere", which cannot be WRITTEN without a decider for `x ≈ y`. That is fatal rather than merely restrictive because `RMod_Forget` lands in `Sets`, so the left adjoint must exist for EVERY setoid, decidable or not — a construction available only at decidable setoids yields no adjunction at all. So encoding 1 is not a trade-off, it is unavailable. The carrier is instead an inductive of formal expressions (`fv_gen`/`fv_zero`/`fv_plus`/`fv_neg`/`fv_smul`) with `fv_eq` an inductive congruence closing under exactly the four abelian-group and four module laws plus saturation under X's `≈` and R's `≈` (Instance/Ab/Tensor.v's shape, reflexivity derived; `fv_Setoid` deliberately NOT an instance, that file's convention). **The list description survives as a THEOREM rather than as the carrier** — `fv_normal_form`: every element is `fv_eq` to a finite formal combination `fv_lc l` — so reordering and collection of like terms are consequences of the congruence rather than obligations on the representation. What that buys, measured: every module law is a constructor, so `FreeModObject` is a record literal with ZERO proof obligations; the setoid constraint `x ≈ y ⟹ e_x = e_y` is the constructor `fe_gen`, making respectfulness of the insertion a one-term proof; and the mediator is a `Fixpoint`, so all three homomorphism laws of the linear extension close by `reflexivity` (recorded as `eq_refl` Examples so the claim is machine-checked rather than inferred from which tactic branch fired). What it costs: seven inductions, of which two carry the universal property (`fv_eval_respects`, 14 cases — eight met by a law of the target module, the other six being generator saturation, congruence and the target setoid's symmetry/transitivity — and `fv_extend_unique`, one case per former) while four more exist only to prove `fv_normal_form`; and **coefficient uniqueness is given up** — hence no decision procedure, no dimension, and NO bridge to Instance/FdVect.v's `FdVectObject`, so it is NOT proved here that the free vector space on a finite generating setoid is finite-dimensional in that file's sense. `free_module_adjunction : FreeMod ⊣ RMod_Forget R` and `free_vect_adjunction : FreeVect F ⊣ Vct_Forget F` are the headline artifacts (via `universal_arrow_from_UMP` → `LeftAdjointFunctorFromUniversalArrows` → `AdjunctionFromUniversalArrows`), with §IV.1's TWO naturality clauses stated and proved in the free module's OWN vocabulary — `free_module_naturality_in_set` (by evaluating at a generator) and `_in_module` (pointwise `reflexivity`) — and then `free_module_naturality_in_set_is_to_adj_nat_l` and its sibling recording by `eq_refl` that those statements ARE the class's fields, a Leibniz equality of statements rather than a parallel restatement. The unit computes (`free_module_unit_is_insert`, `_is_generator`) and the forward transpose IS `fv_transpose` (`free_module_transpose_is_adj`), all `eq_refl`; the COUNIT does not, routing through the `Qed`-opaque `ump_universal_arrows`, and `fmap[FreeMod] u` on a generator is `≈` only since `LeftAdjointFunctorFromUniversalArrows` defines `fmap` by factorization — both pinned as `Fail` probes. **Vect/Free.v specializes by CONVERSION, not by transport**: `Vct_F F = RMod (field_ring F)` by `eq_refl` (it is a `Definition`), every vector-space statement inhabited by its `RMod`-level term, and the field layer buys EXACTLY ONE thing — `field_one_neq_zero` is a field of `FieldObject`, so `1 ≉ 0` is discharged for free; nothing else spends `finv` or commutativity, and an earlier header sentence claiming otherwise was corrected. Non-vacuity at THREE prices, each proved and witnessed over ℤ and ℚ: `free_module_scalars_faithful` needs NO hypothesis, `free_module_two_independent` (the basis is linearly independent — the §III.1 statement a reader wants) needs the decider ONLY, and the reason it can drop `1 ≉ 0` is precisely that over the zero ring every scalar is already 0, so it degenerates there to a truth rather than to a falsehood and survives unassisted, its content carried entirely by the nondegenerate case; `free_module_basis_injective` — which IS false over the zero ring — needs decider AND `1 ≉ 0`. That both hypotheses are unavoidable is ARGUED in the section header, not proved: no converse is machine-checked, and the tree's precedent for exactly that claim shape (`arrow_mul_respects_forces_UIP` and siblings) is deliberately not followed
- **Instance/Mod/Tensor.v**: Mac Lane §III.1 construction 6 + Riehl §2.3 Example 2.3.8 — the tensor product as a UNIVERSAL ELEMENT of the bilinear-maps functor, with `Instance/Vect/Tensor.v` the vector-space reading. `RBilinear V V' W` (additive in each variable, commuting with the action in each), `TensorMod V V' : RModObject R`, `Bilin : RMod R ⟶ Sets` as a genuine functor (action by postcomposition), and the headline `tensor_universal_element : AUniversalElement (Bilin V V') (TensorMod V V')` over Theory/Universal/Element.v's class. **The generality is an arbitrary `RingObject` with NO commutativity hypothesis** — verified at the signatures, not asserted: `TensorMod`, `RBilinear`, `Bilin`, `tensor_med`, `tensor_hom_ext` and `tensor_universal_element` all take a bare `{R : RingObject}`, so the universal element lives in `RMod R` where V and V' do, which is what makes the vector-space reading a conversion rather than a transport (`VctBilinear_is_RBilinear` by `eq_refl`). What that generality COSTS is stated in the right direction and PROVED, not argued: two-sided bilinearity forces the commutators of R to annihilate every bilinear image (`rbl_commutator`: `(r·s)·β(v,w) ≈ (s·r)·β(v,w)`, with `rbl_commutator_annihilates` and `tensor_commutator`), so over a non-commutative ring this object collapses further than a reader of the commutative case expects — and the vacuity half is machine-checked too, `rbl_commutator_from_commutativity` deriving the same identity from commutativity ALONE with no bilinearity and no additivity, so over a commutative ring the identity carries no information. The converse ("vacuous exactly where R is commutative") is FALSE by trivial modules and is not claimed. Balancedness survives as a theorem, `tensor_balanced : (r·v) ⊗ w ≈ v ⊗ (r·w)`. Built as a direct inductive setoid quotient in Instance/Ab/Tensor.v's and Instance/Sets/Coend.v's shape rather than through #305's free module — `MTerm` has 5 formers and `mt_eq` 18 rules against Ab/Tensor.v's 4 and 11 (the formal scalar former, the four module laws, and the two scalar-action-on-generators rules whose composite IS the balanced law are all new), and NO code is shared with that file, only the design pattern. Strengths measured strict-first: the mediator agrees with β on generators definitionally (`tensor_med_gen`), and — in contrast with Instance/Mod/Free.v, whose counit does not compute because `ump_universal_arrows` is `Qed`-opaque — the mediator extracted from the CLASS is the fixpoint on the nose (`tensor_factor_is_med`), the universal element's own element is `tensor_gen`, and the ℤ witnesses compute (2⊗3 ↦ 6). Four `Fail` probes locate the boundary: formal-sum commutativity and the balanced law are not definitional even at closed ℤ scalars, the ℚ mediator's value is not Leibniz-equal (`Qmult (1#2) (4#1)` reduces to `4#2`, so the boundary is the scalar setoid rather than the mediator — the ℤ form of the same statement DOES hold strictly), and the triangle is a value equality but not a record equality. Non-vacuity pins the object exactly rather than gesturing: `Int_tensor_iso : ℤ ⊗_ℤ ℤ ≅ ℤ` in `RMod Int_Ring` and `Q_tensor_iso : ℚ ⊗_ℚ ℚ ≅ ℚ` in `Vct_F Q_Field`, with negative results (`1⊗1 ≉ 0`, `1⊗1 ≉ 2⊗1`) obtained by mapping OUT through `tensor_med_respects`, since no induction on `mt_eq` could give a negative. DEFERRED and disclosed: the collapse is proved as a general identity but is NOT witnessed strict at a concrete non-commutative ring, because the tree has none (every `RingObject` in it is commutative and `EndRig` yields a rig, there being no `EndRing`); no bifunctoriality and no monoidal structure on `RMod R` (Ab/Tensor.v's `AbTensor_Functor` has no counterpart here); no coefficient uniqueness; no comparison with `Instance/Ab/Tensor.v` at ℤ (the ℤ-module structure of an `AbObject` is not in the tree); and the right⊗left→`Ab` reading over a non-commutative ring is explicitly NOT delivered, being a different universal property whose universal element would live in a different category from V and V'. Distinct from `Instance/FdVect/Tensor.v`, which is the diagonal square endofunctor `TensorSq` and not a tensor of two modules; no name collides
- **Instance/FdVect.v**: Finite-dimensional vector spaces and the matrix-category equivalence (Mac Lane §I.4 Ex. 6, Riehl Example 1.5.12) — `FieldObject` over commutative rings (total Proper `finv` with the law on nonzero elements only, `field_no_zero_divisors` derived; owned here per #258's QA-correction) with `Q_Field` and `Field_CRng`; `Vct_F F := RMod (field_ring F)`; `FdVectObject` carrying the chosen basis as a coordinate isomorphism (`fdv_coord`/`fdv_expand` with round trips and linearity — Zorn and decidable linear dependence both avoided, and since the library's ∃ is Type-valued, naming the witness is the same data, not a weakening); `FdVect F` IS Riehl's intermediate category of based spaces with arbitrary linear maps — the factorization's SECOND leg (based → bare fd spaces) is VACUOUS rather than omitted, the Type-valued ∃ making the unbased fd category `FdVect` again, and `FdVect_Forget` (into ALL F-modules, so NOT that leg and no surjectivity claim) is Full+Faithful, recording that carried coordinates behave as mere PROPERTY relative to arbitrary linear maps; `StdVect`/`std_basis` via Matr's `delta`; `Matr_to_FdVect` with the orientation pinned by discriminating eq_refl probes ([[1,2],[3,4]]·(5,6) = (17,39); the transposed reading `mat_apply_T` machine-checked to (23,34) and discriminated from it) and `fmap_comp` = Matr's associativity read one dimension down; `Faithful` + `Full` (`matrix_of g` = columns g(e_j), `matrix_of_sur` by basis expansion through `msum`) + `EssentiallySurjective` (`StdVect_iso` from the coordinate round trips; the instance `#[local]` — chosen-preimage choices stay out of the global database); **`FdVect_Matr_Equivalence` := `FF_ESO_Equivalence`**, axiom-free, with `equivalence_inverse_dim` (the quasi-inverse's object part IS `fdv_dim`, by eq_refl) and ℚ² acceptance incl. the independent `std_reindex` swap-matrix probe. Honest strength, disclosed in-file: the equivalence spends only COMMUTATIVITY of the base ring (twice on its path — `mat_apply_smul`, `matrix_of_sur`), so it would restate verbatim over any commutative ring via `Field_CRng`; `finv`/`field_one_neq_zero` are class content exercised by `finv_r`/`field_no_zero_divisors`/ℚ, not premises. Import-order gotcha recorded: `Coq.QArith.QArith` must precede `Category.Lib` (its `equiv` export shadows Setoid's)
- **Instance/FdVect/DoubleDual.v**: The double-dual natural isomorphism (Mac Lane §I.4 remark 2 + §II.2 construction 3, Awodey §7.5 Ex 7.12, Riehl §1.4 Ex 1.4.4(i)-(ii)) — `DualMod` (the hom-module V* = linear maps into `Ring_RMod`), `Dual : (Vct_F F)^op ⟶ Vct_F F` on ALL F-modules (§II.2's named artifact, no finiteness), `DoubleDual := Dual ◯ Dual^op` (StarAutonomous.v:252's shape), evaluation `eta` with **`double_dual_natural` : Id ⟹ DoubleDual at ANY dimension** — Riehl's scope delivered, not narrowed: only INVERTIBILITY needs finite dimension, and that is **`double_dual_iso`** (per-V `IsIsomorphism (eta V)` on FdVect via the dual basis, which IS `DualFdObj`'s own carried basis — `dual_basis_coord` identifies it with the coordinate functionals); `DualFd : (FdVect F)^op ⟶ FdVect F`; `fd_dual_pointwise_iso` (V ≅ V* pointwise, basis-dependent — Awodey's contrast, the naturality refutation living in NonNatural.v); header records the four uses of `field_comm` and why no `StarAutonomous` instance follows (no monoidal structure on `Vct_F` in tree); `rmod_hom_neg` added generically (Mod.v's Preadditive lacks negation). All axiom-free. **Instance/FdVect/NonNatural.v**: the negative half — a transformation `Id ⟹ DualFd` is not even well-typed (the variance clash IS Riehl's clean statement, recorded in the header), so the well-posed reading is the Character/NonNatural.v precedent: covariant through inversion on the iso-core, and `sigma_not_natural` refutes ONE isomorphism ℚ¹ ≅ (ℚ¹)* at the SINGLE pointwise square of the automorphism 2·(−) (2q ≈ ½q forces q ≈ 0, then σ collapses e₁ into 0 — Mac Lane's m² ≢ 1 obstruction over ℚ), with `sigma_categorical_not_natural` bridging from the categorical square by `exact` (DualFd's fmap IS precomposition definitionally) and `sigma_family_not_natural` the family form — whose premise, UNLIKE the character precedent's, is proven INHABITED and machine-checked to slot in (`family_premise_inhabited` repackages `fd_dual_pointwise_iso` at the FdVect hom-level, the homs being the same `RModHom`s, and `family_not_natural_applies` feeds it to the corollary): pointwise isos exist at every object, and what is refuted is exactly their naturality — the hypothesis consumed is ONE equation, the square at the single point (e₁, e₁). **Instance/FdVect/Tensor.v**: Riehl Ex 1.4.4(vii) (filed by doc/plan/books/qa/DECISIONS.md §4.1) — the diagonal square functor `TensorSq` (object `StdVect F (n²)`, morphisms by the Kronecker action through `fentry`/`hom_coords`, functoriality via the `fin_sum_split`/`fin_sum_pair`/`fin_sum_pair_mul`/`delta_pair` index kit over FinSet/Product.v's codec — upstreaming candidates beside `fin_sum_swap`), and **`diag_transform_zero`**: every `Id[FdVect F] ⟹ TensorSq` is the zero family, under explicit `1+1 ≉ 0` (the scaling square at 2·(−) gives 2x ≈ 4x and `finv` cancels the 2 — the first place #244's field layer is LOAD-BEARING), ℚ instance `diag_transform_zero_Q`, with `tsq_zero_transform` proving the zero family IS natural (the classification is of a nonempty collection); header contrasts `Collapse.v:526`'s abstract `no_cloning` (assumes a natural cocommutative diagonal in a compact closed category; neither implies the other)
- **Instance/Matr/Determinant.v**: The determinant over a commutative ring, in full — first-column Laplace `det` over `Matr`'s setoid matrices with the `fin_delete`/`minor`/`pow_neg1` kit and explicit unfolding rules (`det_S`/`fin_sum_S` — plain `simpl` over-unfolds `fin_sum`), `det_id`, `det_map` (ring homs commute with the polynomial formula, negation by `RigHom_neg`), and the multiplicativity chain with NO symmetric-group enumeration: column multilinearity → first-ROW Laplace `det_row_expand` (making adjacent-equal-columns alternation a two-term cancellation, no involution pairing) → swap-negation → general alternation `det_alt` (NOT derived from det M ≈ −det M, which is vacuous in characteristic 2) → `det_perm` for EVERY index function (non-injective killed by decidable `fin_search`) → nested-sum product expansion → `det_mul`/`det_compose`; then both Laplace expansions (`det_transpose`, cycle-based `det_col_expand`, alien-cofactor `det_col_alien`) give the adjugate `adj` with `adj_mul_l`/`adj_mul_r` and invertibility both ways (`det_unit_of_iso`, `iso_of_det_unit`); eq_refl acceptance tests at 2×2/3×3 (det [[1,2],[3,4]] = −2, adjugate entries, det∘compose = product) and hypothesis-free ℤ exports; reusable ring-free index combinatorics (`fswap` kit, `fin_search`, and `all_fins` with completeness/NoDup — disclosed as the tree's third copy of the Fin enumeration alongside FinSet/Skeleton.v's `fin_enum` and FinSet/Pushout.v's `all_fin`, re-derived to avoid a cross-hierarchy dependency, with the NoDup helper renamed `map_FS_NoDup` to avoid Skeleton.v's name) via per-lemma `Proof using Type`; invertibility also in existential form (`invertible_of_det_unit`). Instance/Matr/GL.v assembles Mac Lane's §I.3–I.4 flagship: `UnitsOf : MonObject → GrpObject` (units-of-a-monoid ONCE — first-component setoid, swap-inverse; `mon_inverse_unique` makes that the right quotient) with `UnitsOf_map`, the units functor `(−)^* : CRng ⟶ Grp` at the multiplicative monoid, `GL_n : CRng ⟶ Grp` as `UnitsOf` of the endomorphism monoid `hom_monoid (Matr K) n` with entrywise functoriality, and `det_Transform : GL_n ⟹ (−)^*` — the naturality square `det_{K'} ∘ GL_n f ≈ f^* ∘ det_K` is `det_map`, Mac Lane's first example of a natural transformation, proven, with the determinant lemmas kept reusable for the matrix-equivalence exercise (`maclane:I.4:ex6`)
- **Instance/Sets.v**: Category of setoids (not strict sets!). Monos and epis are both characterized at one universe: `injectivity_is_monic`, and `surjectivity_is_epic` with its projections `surjective_implies_epic` / `epic_implies_surjective`, yielding `Sets_balanced` (monic + epic ⇒ iso) through the existing `bijective_is_iso`. The epi converse had been abandoned mid-proof for years on the belief that it inherited the subobject-classifier size obstruction; it does not. The classical probe does need a truth-value object with carrier `Type`, which is genuinely too big, but the **cokernel pair** `carrier B + carrier B` glued along the image tests the same thing and stays at the universe of the carriers (`Im`/`ckrel`/`CKSetoid`). The classifier obstruction proper is untouched and still real — Instance/Sets/Classifier.v remains cross-universe theorems rather than an instance — and now also records `lifted_epic_implies_epic`/`epic_implies_lifted_epic`, i.e. that `Setoid_Lift` is inert for epicness. All constructive: `∃` is `sigT`, so preimages are data and no choice principle is used. `Instance/FinSet/Classifier.v`'s `finset_epic_iff_surjective` needs no substitute probe at all, `Ω := 2` being an honest object of the category, with acceptance tests including the negative `finset_const_true_not_epic`
- **Instance/Grp/TwoFunctors.v**: Mac Lane §I.3's exercise — two functors that share the identity object function and differ on arrows, distinctness proved in `Functor_StrictEq_Setoid` (the `StrictCat` hom-setoid), because `Cat`'s weak `Functor_Setoid` identifies naturally isomorphic functors and so cannot state it. The machine is `Functor/Twist.v`: for any family `α : ∀ x, x ≅ F x` with naturality NOT assumed, `Twist F α` is an endofunctor whose object function is literally `fun x => x`; it is strictly equal to `Id` exactly when α is natural (`Twist_natural_strict_id`, with the converse under UIP on objects), yet for `F = Id` it is ALWAYS naturally isomorphic to `Id` (`Twist_Id_weak_equiv`) — the strict/weak seam in one construction. Group witness: `S3` (nonabelian, semidirect presentation over a decidable carrier) with inner automorphisms `Grp_conj`/`Grp_conj_iso`, twisted over `GrpAt S3`, the one-object full subcategory of `Grp` on S3 (inclusion full, faithful, injective on objects), separated on the concrete morphism `S3_conj_r` at the element `S3_s`. The degenerate cases are PROVED degenerate rather than avoided by assertion: conjugation over an abelian group is the identity (`Grp_conj_abelian`, instantiated at `Z2`), and the canonical uniform family — inversion into the opposite group — is natural, so its twist is an endofunctor of the WHOLE of `Grp` with the identity object function that is strictly equal to `Id` (`Grp_op_twist_is_Id`). **The issue's own ask — a distinct pair over all of `Grp` — is NOT delivered**; no such family is constructed; the classical route needs a decision procedure for object equality, which costs `classic` plus `constructive_indefinite_description`. **Construction/Free/TwoFunctors.v** is the group-free companion (Fong–Spivak §3.3.2 Ex. 3.40): the free category on one arrow to the free category on two parallel arrows, the same object function, different images of the generator, distinctness again strict
- **Instance/Sets/Products.v**: Small products and coproducts in `Sets` — the tree's FIRST `HasIndexedProducts` instance at any category (dependent-function setoids `∀ i, F i` under pointwise `≈`) and the first `HasIndexedCoproducts` instance (sigma setoids `{ i : A & F i }`, two elements identified by an equality of indices transporting one to the other); both funext-free by the setoid discipline. The smallness side condition is carried by UNIVERSES, not by hypothesis: `Structure/Limit/Product.v`'s existing class is instantiated AS STATED, with no variant — `{A : Type}` is a universe PARAMETER of the class, not a quantifier over all universes — and `Sets_HasIndexedProducts@{u u0} : HasIndexedProducts@{u0 u0 u0 u0 u u0} Sets@{u0 u}` identifies all three of the class's index universes with `o` — the universe of the CARRIERS of `Sets`' objects, where the small sets live. NOT with the universe of `obj[Sets]`: `Sets@{o so} : Category@{so o o}`, so qua `Category` its objects live at `so`, strictly above `o`, and indexing by `obj[Sets]` is precisely the universe inconsistency named next. The condition genuinely bites, and precisely: a family indexed by the objects of the SAME `Sets` is a universe inconsistency, and the unpinned form typechecks only because Coq silently uses a STRICTLY SMALLER `Sets` for the index (`obj[Sets@{u0 u}] → obj[Sets@{u u1}]` with `u0 < u < u1`). The one index a class was previously refused for — the endomorphism-indexed product of `Theory/WeaklyInitial.v` — is shown ABSENT as an obstacle at `Sets`: `Sets_endo_iprod`/`Sets_endo_iprod_ump` build `∏_{u : P ~> P} P` WITH ITS UMP from this very instance, at the same `Sets@{u0 u}`, no universe growth. The general note stands unchanged and is quoted verbatim in the header, since for an arbitrary category the hom universe need not sit where the class puts the index. `HasIndexedCoproducts` is NEW vocabulary (`Structure/Limit/Coproduct.v`), defined as `@HasIndexedProducts (C^op)` in the house duality idiom with covariant accessors and no proof obligation of its own. Awodey §2.9 Ex 7(b) lands as `Sets_exponent_IsIndexedProduct`/`Sets_constant_iprod_exponent` (`X^I ≅ ∏_{i∈I} X` at the discrete setoid on the index). Non-degeneracy is checked rather than asserted: `Sets_iprod_bool`/`_icoprod_bool` recover the binary (co)products of `Instance/Sets/{Cartesian,Cocartesian}.v`, `Sets_iprod_nat`/`_icoprod_nat` give the stream and cons decompositions at an infinite index, the empty and singleton cases are PROVED and labelled degenerate, and `Sets_iprod_unit_pigeonhole` / `Sets_iprod_nat_no_pigeonhole` (plus coproduct duals) SEPARATE them — one and the same three-element pigeonhole statement, proved at a singleton index and refuted at `nat`. Header discloses the deferred Mac Lane §III.4 `Top` extension (optional, gated on #259, not a blocker)
- **Instance/Sets/Complete.v**: `Sets_Complete : @Complete Sets` — the FIRST `Complete`/`Cocomplete` instance in the library, hence the first concrete witness for a premise of GAFT/SAFT and `Comma_Complete`. The limit of `F : D ⟶ Sets` is the setoid of compatible families, a sub-setoid of `Sets_iprod_obj (fun d => F d)`; cone coherence IS the compatibility constraint. The header states plainly that this is a DIRECT construction and NOT a routing through "products + equalizers ⇒ complete" — that theorem is not in this tree (`Complete_HasEqualizers`, `Adjunction/GAFT.v:193`, runs the other way). Smallness again by universes: `Sets_Complete@{u u0} : Complete@{u u u u0}` puts both the diagram category's objects and its homs at `o`, the universe of the carriers of `Sets`' objects (`Sets@{o so} : Category@{so o o}`), strictly below where `obj[Sets]` lives. `Cocomplete Sets` is NOT provided. This is the premise that was MISSING, not the only one outstanding: `GAFT`'s other two are `PreservesImageLimit U`, discharged for EVERY right adjoint by the pre-existing `right_adjoint_PreservesImageLimit` (`Construction/Comma/Limit.v`), and `SolutionSet U d`, constructible — so with `Sets_Complete` in hand all three are met at once and `Adjunction/GAFT/Sets.v` runs `GAFT`; docs/INHABITATION.md moves the row up to *Witnessed results* accordingly
- **Instance/Sets/Quotient.v**, **Instance/Sets/Quotient/Partition.v**, **Instance/Sets/Coequalizer.v**, **Instance/Sets/Coequalizer/Interconnect.v**: Mac Lane §III.1 construction 4 + §III.3 remark 2 and Ex 5, Awodey §3.4 Example 3.19 / §3.5 Ex 4, Seven Sketches §1.2.1 Def 1.21 and §6.2.4 Example 6.42 — quotient setoids and **`Sets_HasCoequalizers`, the tree's FIRST `HasCoequalizers` inhabitant at any category** (measured: only the class at `Structure/Coequalizer.v:68`, the DERIVED `HasCoequalizers_HasReflexiveCoequalizers`, and prose existed). **THE TWO PRESENTATIONS OF THE HYPOTHESIS ARE THE SAME ONE, AND THAT IS A THEOREM**: the issue asks separately for a quotient by a “setoid-respecting” relation and for one by a relation “coarser than `≈`”, and `coarser_iff_respects` proves them equivalent for an EQUIVALENCE relation, each direction consuming exactly one law (`coarser_respects` transitivity, `respects_coarser` reflexivity), so ONE construction `SetsQuotient A R HR` is built rather than two. Finer measurement: the quotient OBJECT needs only `Equivalence R`, and `SetoidCoarser` enters exactly at the PROJECTION. The house carrier discipline holds — same carrier, coarser `≈` — and `sets_quot_finest_eq : SetsQuotient A equiv A = A` closes by `eq_refl` on the **WHOLE RECORD**. The universal property is an `AUniversalElement (RespFunctor R)` over Theory/Universal/Element.v's class; **the Yoneda route REFUSES at `Sets`** (that file's donor restriction identifies object, hom and proof universes while `Sets@{o so} : Category@{so o o}`), pinned as two `Fail` probes with positive controls, so the Yoneda-free `ue_representation`/`AUniversalElement_of_repr` route carries `sets_quot_Representable`. **Mac Lane §III.3 Ex 5 lands ON THE NOSE**: `SetsRelObj X R` is the triples ⟨x,y,w⟩ with `w : R x y` compared by `≈` in the first two components only (the witness is data the equality ignores), and `sets_rel_IsCoequalizer` exhibits the quotient ITSELF — not something isomorphic to it — as the coequalizer of the two projections, with `sets_rel_coeq_iso` relating it to the generated-closure coequalizer by the identity on carriers both ways; it is NOT claimed to be a subobject of `X × X` (no map to the product is built and nothing is proved monic). **Seven Sketches' partition presentation hits a GENUINE UNIVERSE WALL, proved by probe rather than asserted**: a `Type@{o}`-valued class object does not exist at the carrier's level, `carrier A → Type@{o}` living at `Type@{o+1}`, pinned as `Fail Definition probe_parts_type_same@{o}` against a `Prop`-valued control at the same level and a `Type`-valued one a level up — and the same wall refutes the impredicative intersection Awodey's §3.5 Ex 4 uses, so THAT header claim is measured too. It is therefore delivered at THREE GRADED STRENGTHS rather than one: `sets_part_of` exists under the quotient's own hypotheses, is surjective, and separates UP TO TRUNCATION; `sets_partition_iso` is an isomorphism in `Sets` under `SquashStable`; and `partition_stability_is_the_conclusion` proves that hypothesis IS injectivity of `sets_part_of`, so no cheaper one exists (the `Instance/Grp/Epi.v` discipline) — with `prop_rel_squash_stable` discharging it for `Prop`-valued relations, which is the book's own setting, so Seven Sketches' identification is recovered with nothing extra. `SetsPartsProp` is the strict reading (a sub-setoid of Powerset.v's `Powerset_Prop_obj`, no representative); its evident inverse would be large elimination out of `Prop`, stated as unavailable BY THAT CANDIDATE with **no impossibility claimed**. The §6.2.4 circuit witness is EVALUATED — six ports, three wires, three terminals (one a lone unconnected port) — with the count PROVED rather than read off (`ic_terminal_cases` exhaustive AND `ic_terminal_distinct` pairwise), the carrier the port type by `eq_refl`, and the mediator produced by `coeq_desc` out of the delivered `IsCoequalizer` computing on closed ports; it is over `Sets` DELIBERATELY, since `FinSet` computes counts but has no `HasCoequalizers` instance, so a witness there would exercise a parallel construction — **nothing is claimed about `FinSet`**. Errata corrected in the file headers: the issue's “`rg -i 'coequalizer' Instance/` returns exactly two hits, both prose” is stale (22 hits in 3 files, including `Instance/Grp/Quotient/Colimit.v`'s REAL `quot_IsCoequalizer`/`normal_closure_IsCoequalizer` from #313) and its `Instance/Two.v:51` is off by four. - **Instance/Sets/Cocomplete.v**, **Instance/Sets/Chain.v**: Mac Lane §III.3 pp. 67-68 and §V.1 Exercise 8 (Riehl 2nd ed. §3.1 Example 3.1.26) — **`Sets_Cocomplete : @Cocomplete Sets`**, the tree's first HYPOTHESIS-FREE cocompleteness witness (the only other `@Cocomplete _` constants — `Proset_Cocomplete_of_all_joins`, `Cocomplete_equivalence_invariant`, `creates_colimits_Cocomplete` — each take one). It is the GENERAL small-colimit witness, `∀ (D : Category) (F : D ⟶ Sets), Colimit F` for EVERY shape, not merely the ω-chain reading the issue's title emphasises — V.1.8 demands the general statement. Construction: `Sets_icoprod_obj` (#254's sigma setoid) quotiented by `colim_rel`, a four-constructor inductive (fibre equivalence, diagram glue, symmetry, transitivity; reflexivity derived — the Coend.v shape), with the apex `SetsQuotient` of the first by the second. **`Sets_HasCoequalizers` is NOT consumed** — the layer beneath it is — so `Instance/Sets/Coequalizer.v:66`'s forecast about what a later cocompleteness proof would need is not what happened; and no "coproducts + coequalizers ⇒ cocomplete" reduction exists in tree in either variance, so this is a direct construction like its `Sets_Complete` mirror. **The universe block is FREER than that mirror, though read "earned" narrowly — what was earned is writing the annotation, and the mirror could have it too: the identification at `Sets_Complete` is ALSO minimization, its body accepting the same annotation in a scratch check, so nothing here says limits are inherently tighter than colimits**: `Sets_Cocomplete@{uc ud uo uso}` carries `ud <= uo` where `Sets_Complete@{u u0} : Complete@{u u u u0}` identifies them — the diagram category's OBJECTS may sit strictly below `Sets`' carrier universe. Two measured facts behind it: the four binders are written out DELIBERATELY (unannotated it minimizes to `ud := uo`, and the identification is minimization rather than a constraint — a declared `Constraint pd < po` instantiation is accepted), and the colimit leg is built DIRECTLY rather than as the donor composite `sets_quot_proj ∘ Sets_icoprod_inj`, because `Sets_icoprod_inj@{u u0}` identifies index and carrier universes and that identification was measured to propagate to the headline. The two legs are nonetheless proved equal at `eq_refl`. **No duality shortcut exists and that is measured, not assumed**: `Colimit F := Limit (F^op)` is definitional, but `F^op` lands in `Sets^op`, not `Sets` — what would give the colimit is `Complete (Sets^op)`, which IS `Cocomplete Sets` again. **NON-VACUITY IS PROVED IN BOTH DIRECTIONS, EACH AT ONE SHAPE — read that scope, since neither is a general non-collapse theorem and both headers refuse to say otherwise.** The quotient does not collapse: at `DiscreteCat bool` with a singleton over each object, `two_fibres_not_collapsed` separates the two fibres by mapping OUT through `colim_rel_separates` to `discriminate` (no induction on the relation could yield a negative), with the degeneracies excluded by proof — `two_disc_objects_distinct`, both fibre points NAMED and appearing in the theorem statement so each typechecks only because its fibre is inhabited, and `two_disc_hom_is_eq` recording at `eq_refl` that the shape's homs are index equality, which is why the discrete case is the sharp one. And the quotient does merge: a discrete shape has no connecting maps and so can witness nothing here, so `omega_stages_merged` / `omega_stages_apart_in_coproduct` (Chain.v) carry that half at the ω-shape. **What the two witnesses JOINTLY establish is that NEITHER endpoint describes `colim_rel` uniformly** — the ω pair refutes "always the coproduct's relation", the discrete witness refutes "always total" — a statement about the FAMILY. It is NOT that the relation sits strictly inside that interval at either shape; measured, it sits AT an endpoint at both (total at `OmegaPoints`, equal to the coproduct's relation at `TwoPoints`). An earlier revision of this bullet said the two ω constants "place `colim_rel` STRICTLY between" the two, which no in-tree theorem supports and which is refutable at both witnesses. The empty-shape reading is included and explicitly labelled a DEGENERATE control that does not stand alone. `Instance/Sets/Chain.v` gives the ω-reading as `Sets_Omega_Colimit` and `Sets_Chain_Colimit`, both bodies pure applications of `Sets_Cocomplete` — Riehl's checkbox asks for a `Colimit` over `Construction/Chain.v`'s shape rather than a bespoke construction, and that is what they are. SCOPE: the union characterisation is ONE-DIRECTIONAL — the sufficient half (`omega_colim_of_stage`) is proved, the NECESSARY half is not, because the converse needs a directedness merge comparing `fmap[G]` at two order proofs. Read that reason precisely: `Instance/Omega.v` itself declares `le_t` with no uniqueness lemma, but the lemma EXISTS in tree — `le_t_irr {m n} (f g : le_t m n) : f = g` at `Instance/Ordinal.v:223`, axiom-free by UIP on `nat`, in a file that requires `Omega` and reuses that same `le_t` — so the deferral is a SCOPE choice, not a missing lemma, and an earlier revision's "that would have to be proved first" was wrong. So the legs are NOT shown injective for inclusion-like chains and "the colimit IS the union" is not claimed. Also not delivered BY THESE FILES: no normal form for `colim_rel`, hence no decision procedure and no filtered-collapse theorem; no comparison with `Sets_HasCoequalizers`, `Sets_HasIndexedCoproducts` as the discrete case, `Instance/Sets/Pushout.v` or `SetsCoend`; no functoriality in F; no `AdamekData`, so `Theory/Adamek/Corollaries.v`'s chain gains a `Cocomplete` witness at `Sets` but stays conditional. `Sets_Chain_Colimit@{u}` is PINNED to `Sets@{Set u}`, attributed to `Construction/Chain.v:64`'s own result type fixing Omega's hom/proof universes at `Set` — inherited, not repaired, not claimed unavoidable. `Test/ProbeCocomplete329.v` guards the boundary with 3 negatives plus an instrument check, rename-simulated at 8/8 over the eight constants in the NEGATIVES — the audit found `Opposite` unguarded in the very expression whose `Opposite_Functor` a first run had already fixed, and found the whole-morphism triangle refutation measured but pinned nowhere; both are now shipped, and the previously claimed "13/13" matched no countable set, and records the probe-hygiene trap that spelling the duality negative as `Sets_Complete (D^op) (F^op)` is a FALSE PASS — `Functor/Opposite.v` opens `functor_scope`, so `D^op` parses as `Opposite_Functor D` and the command fails on notation rather than on the mathematics
- **Instance/Sets/Pullback.v**: Mac Lane §III.4 Exercises 1 and 6 (book p. 72) + Awodey §5.2 (construction and Example 5.9) + Riehl §3.2 Example 3.2.9 — pullbacks and kernel pairs in `Sets`. **`Sets_HasPullbacks : HasPullbacks Sets`** is the agreement sub-setoid of the product carrier with its projections and `∃!` mediator, and is the second inhabitant of `HasPullbacks` **FOR A CONCRETE CATEGORY** — read that qualifier, because unqualified the claim is false and this very commit refutes it: three generic conditionals inhabit `HasPullbacks C` (`HasPullbacks_of_Cartesian_HasEqualizers`, `HasPullbacks_op_of_HasPushouts`, `codomain_cleaving_pullbacks`), and the accompanying probe itself builds a second `HasPullbacks Sets` out of the first of them. `FinSet_Pullbacks` (`Instance/FinSet/Classifier.v:264`) was the only prior concrete one, and it lives in the finite SKELETAL model where objects are naturals; `Instance/Sets/Classifier.v:226`'s `sets_char_pullback` proves ONE square is a pullback, not that arbitrary cospans have them, and it sits one universe up. **THE ISSUE'S SUGGESTED SHORTCUT WAS MEASURED AND DECLINED, AND THE MEASUREMENT IS THE INTERESTING PART**: it proposes reading the instance off #326's `HasPullbacks_of_Cartesian_HasEqualizers` now that that has landed, and the derivation DOES elaborate — but the derived apex is **transparent and wrong**, not opaque: it reduces on the nose to `Sets_limit_obj (APair (f ∘ exl) (g ∘ exr))`, the compatible-family setoid over the walking parallel pair, so an element is a dependent function plus a constraint quantified over every arrow of `Parallel` rather than a PAIR (`Fail … DerObj = sets_pb_obj f g` gives `cannot unify`). Since `Sets_HasEqualizers := Complete_HasEqualizers Sets_Complete` is itself read off completeness, that was predictable; it is now measured. The build is therefore DIRECT, and #326's reduction is used the other way round, for the Riehl deliverable. That measurement is pinned in `Test/ProbeSetsPullback333.v` rather than in the file, because stating it needs `Adjunction/GAFT/Sets.v` and requiring that from the target would put the whole GAFT closure behind every consumer of `Sets` pullbacks. **Mac Lane Ex 6 in full**: `sets_kernel_pair_obj` records `Pull _ _ (kernel_pair f) = sets_ker f` at LEIBNIZ `=` (with `_fst`/`_snd` likewise), so `Structure/Regular.v:46`'s generic `kernel_pair` IS the relation setoid `{(x,x′) | f x ≈ f x′}` on the nose, and the equivalence-relation reading is given AS ARROWS — `sets_ker_refl : a ~> sets_ker f`, `sets_ker_sym : sets_ker f ~> sets_ker f`, `sets_ker_trans : sets_ker_comp f ~> sets_ker f` — with all six leg equations and `sets_ker_comp_is_pullback` by `eq_refl`. **Awodey Example 5.9** is `sets_preimage_IsPullback` (the pullback of a subset inclusion `V ↪ B` along `f` is `{a | f a ∈ V}`) together with the elementwise criterion `sets_preimage_criterion : FactorsThrough (sub_incl (sets_preimage f S)) t ↔ FactorsThrough (sub_incl S) (f ∘ t)` — kept `Defined` rather than `Qed` deliberately, since with `Qed` the downstairs factorization does not reduce and the witness evaluations fail. **Riehl's follow-on**, `sets_pullback_is_equalizer` with `sets_equalizer_is_pullback` back, is the pullback AS the equalizer of `f ∘ exl` and `g ∘ exr`. **Read that novelty precisely** — the issue calls this "nowhere stated in ANY category" and cites `Structure/Topos.v:23` as recording a known gap; BOTH are stale, and stale because of #326: that header now says the redundancy "is now a CHOICE rather than a gap" and that the reduction AND its converse "ARE formalized", naming `equalizer_of_pullback` (`Structure/Pullback/Reduction.v:287`). What is genuinely new here is the first INSTANTIATION at a concrete category — outside `Structure/` and `Test/` the only other `IsEqualizer` term is `matr_IsEqualizer_op` (`Instance/Matr/Coequalizer.v:328`), an op-read of a matrix COequalizer. Non-vacuity computes and is not degenerate: over the graph of `Nat.even`, the mediator evaluates (`sets_pb_snd (even_med 5) = false` by `eq_refl`); `even_med_is_ump` shows the `ump_pullbacks` mediator IS `sets_pb_med` on the nose but carries NO non-vacuity content, since the GENERAL equation at an arbitrary cospan also holds by `eq_refl` (measured) — so it would hold at degenerate cospans too, and the general form a consumer would want is NOT stated; `sets_ker evenM` relates 0 and 2 with `even_ker_not_diagonal` proving the relation is NOT the diagonal, and `(0,2)∘(2,4) = (0,4)` computes; the preimage side factors a non-constant map and carries it downstairs by the criterion, with `constThree_does_not_factor` as the negative. Universes are clean: `Sets_HasPullbacks@{u u0}` carries only `Sets`' own `u0 < u` — no identification, no `Set` — and even the concrete witnesses stay polymorphic by going through `eq_Setoid` (`Lib/Setoid.v:65`) rather than resolving `eq_equivalence` at an unannotated binder. 103/103 constants closed under the global context (95 glob `def|prf|inst|proj|rec` + 7 `_obligation_` constants that `Print Module` lists and the glob does not + `Build_SubsetOf`, which appears in neither). The probe carries **4 negatives of TWO kinds** — three CONVERSION with distinct causes (the derivation-route mismatch above; the pairing failing to round-trip strictly because stdlib `prod` has no definitional eta; and the equalizer round trip at MORPHISM level, which the target's header called measured while an earlier revision of the probe pinned it nowhere) and one TYPING (the bundled `Pullback` record carries its apex as data, so it is not applicable to an apex and two legs — `Illegal application`, a donor fact `Theory/Morphisms/CokernelPair.v` already pins for the dual) — plus an instrument check, each stripped and its kind confirmed, rename-simulated **10/10** on an unpadded denominator. NOT delivered: the `Top` half of Exercise 1, gated on #259 and recorded as an extension; no `Sets` pushout/pullback interaction; no stability or pasting results beyond what `Theory/Morphisms/Stability.v` already gives
- **Instance/Sets/Powerset.v**: The covariant and contravariant power-set functors on `Sets` (Mac Lane §I.3's first worked example of a functor, with the §II.2 inverse-image companion) over TWO carriers of subsets, because one carrier cannot do both jobs. (1) The PROOF-RELEVANT carrier `Powerset_obj X` — the `≈`-respecting `Type@{o}`-valued predicates under pointwise `↔`, i.e. the hom-setoid `Setoid_Lift X ~> PropSetoid` assembled from Instance/Sets/Classifier.v's donors with no new record — whose placement is honestly CROSS-UNIVERSE and carried in the types: `Powerset_obj@{o so} : SetoidObject@{o o} → SetoidObject@{so so}` with `o < so` (note `o` is the universe of the CARRIERS of `Sets`' objects, not the object universe, which is `so` — `Sets@{o so} : Category@{so o o}`), so `Powerset : Sets@{o so} ⟶ Sets@{so sso}` (direct image `∃ x, S x ∧ f x ≈ y`) and `Powerset_op : (Sets@{o so})^op ⟶ Sets@{so sso}` (inverse image `λ y, T (f y)`) are NOT endofunctors, and the singleton family is `Powerset_Singleton : Sets_Lift ⟹ Powerset` over the carrier-preserving lift. The consumer consequence is disclosed in the file: `@Monad Sets Powerset` and `FAlg Powerset` are BOTH rejected by the universe checker (`Theory/Monad.v:90` and `Construction/FAlg.v:114` each require an endofunctor), so the power-set monad (#466) and any initial-algebra reading (#750) cannot ride on this carrier and `Powerset_Singleton` cannot serve as a monad unit. (2) The TRUNCATED carrier `Powerset_Prop_obj X` — the same subsets valued in `Prop`, the direct-image existential truncated impredicatively by `Powerset_squash A := ∀ Q : Prop, (A → Q) → Q` — sits at the SAME universe as the carriers (its one cost is `Set < o`, since `Prop : Type@{Set+1}`), giving a genuine ENDOfunctor `Powerset_Prop : Sets ⟶ Sets` together with Riehl §1.4's singleton transformation in its own shape, `Powerset_Prop_Singleton : Id[Sets] ⟹ Powerset_Prop`, naturality proved; both `@Monad Sets Powerset_Prop` and `FAlg Powerset_Prop` are formable over it and are entered into the environment as `Powerset_Prop_Monad_statement`/`Powerset_Prop_FAlg` so the claim is type-checked rather than asserted (neither the monad nor an initial algebra is BUILT — #466 and #750 stay open). The trade-off is exactly proof relevance versus an endofunctor downstream consumers can use, and `Powerset_truncate : Powerset ⟹ Powerset_Prop_Lift` is the comparison from the first carrier to the second (`Powerset_Prop_Lift` is `Sets_Lift ◯ Powerset_Prop` assembled by hand, since `Compose` shares one hom universe across its three categories; two `reflexivity` lemmas check it IS that composite). SCOPE THE IMPOSSIBILITY — an earlier version of this entry said Riehl's `Id ⟹ P` is NOT STATABLE in this library, and that is RETRACTED both here and in the file's header. It is statable. The rejection holds only for the `Type@{o}`-valued carrier; the inference "`≈` is `Type@{o}`-valued, THEREFORE subsets are" is a non sequitur, since a `Prop`-valued predicate respects a `Type`-valued `≈` fine (`Type@{o} → Prop` is a `Prop` by impredicativity), and Instance/Sets/Classifier.v's obstruction is witness RECOVERY for a pullback UMP (its own note, :138-142), which a power set does not owe. In-tree precedent for `Prop`-valued subsets at the carrier's own level: `Instance/Ens.v:35` and `Instance/Rel.v:47`. Funext-free by design and axiom-free. Non-vacuity is PROVEN rather than asserted, including the meta-point: `Powerset_injective_reflects` shows that along an injective map the direct image merges nothing, so an injective witness testifies to nothing; `Powerset_merges_fibre` is an iff (singletons merge EXACTLY on the fibres of `f`) with the corollary `Powerset_not_injective_on_subsets`; `Fin.t 2`/`Fin.t 3` witnesses show the direct image moves subsets (`Powerset_image_moves`), differs from the inverse image on the same input (`Powerset_direct_ne_inverse`), and cannot grow a singleton the way the inverse image does (`fin32_preimage_sng0` against `fin3_sub01_not_singleton`); and `Powerset_Prop_singletons_distinct` shows the truncation does not collapse the power set
- **Instance/Coq.v**: Category of Coq types and functions
- **Instance/Coq/Monoid/Free.v**: The free monoid and its universal property (Mac Lane §II.7 Cor 2; Awodey §1.7 construction + Props 1.9/1.10, §2.9 Ex 4, §7.5 Ex 7.7, §9.1, §9.9 Ex 2; Seven Sketches §5.2.3 Ex 5.24 — NINE catalog items in one file). Lists over X as an object of `Mon` at `Coq` (no new structure needed — `Coq_Monoidal` was already in tree), with `free_mmul_is_app`/`free_mone_is_nil` pinning concatenation and the empty word by `eq_refl`; the fold `free_ext` with `free_ext_unique`; **`free_monoid_universal`** (the ∃! statement — the issue's pinned name, resolving verbatim) packaged through `Theory/Universal/Arrow.v`'s `universal_arrow_from_UMP` as `free_monoid_universal_arrow`, and thence `free_monoid_adjunction : FreeMonoid ⊣ UMon` with its transposition (`free_monoid_transposition`/`_at`/`_inv`) and `free_monoid_unit_is_insert` by `eq_refl`. Awodey §7.5's insertion of generators is a genuine `Transform` (`insert_Transform : Id[Coq] ⟹ UMon ◯ FreeMonoid`) whose naturality square IS `free_fmap_generators` — "the induced homomorphism is determined by its action on generators" made literal — with `free_fmap_is_map` identifying the free functor's action with `List.map`. §2.9 Ex 4's `A`-Mon comma reading is delivered both ways (`free_monoid_initial` / `AMon_initial_universal_arrow`, with `eq_refl` round-trip checks), and Seven Sketches' two-generator computation identifies the pre-existing `ListMon` (Construction/Funny/Comparison.v) as an INSTANCE rather than a coincidence (`ListMon_hom_is_free_carrier`/`_id_is_free_unit`/`_compose_is_free_mul`, all `eq_refl`, the last pinning the diagrammatic argument order), with concrete words incl. `word_noncommutative` and folds into an XOR monoid. The `list_initial` bridge is `free_ext_is_fold`/`_is_initial_algebra_map`/`_is_the_unique_algebra_map` — the fold IS the initial-algebra map. **Awodey §9.9 Ex 2 is delivered SCOPED, and the scope is the honest content**: `adjunction_counit_underlying_retraction` (generic, hypothesis-free — `U ε` is split by the unit, one triangle identity) and `adjunction_counit_epic` (generic, under `Faithful U`), instantiated for the free monoid; what is NOT claimed is that the counit is a split epi IN `Mon`, because the evident section `m ↦ [m]` is not a homomorphism — the exercise's "every monoid is a quotient of a free one" is the surjection of UNDERLYING SETS, which is what is proved. This is new vocabulary: `adj_monic` (Theory/Adjunction.v) was the only statement of that family, with no epi counterpart. 72 named constants / 86 with obligations, all closed. **Theory/Universal/Arrow.v gains the uniqueness statement a CONSUMER wants**, and the file is precise about what was and was not already there (the issue's own QA note is inaccurate on this point and the file corrects it): `initial_unique_up_to_unique_iso` (Structure/Initial.v) DOES carry out the generic initial-object argument — but it lives in the comma `=(c) ↓ F` with its uniqueness clause ranging over COMMA isomorphisms and predicate `True`. Read down in D, where the object of interest is `arrow_obj` rather than the comma pair, that predicate would be FALSE: `arrow_obj` can have automorphisms (the free monoid on two generators has one exchanging them), and those are isomorphisms between the two universal objects that do NOT commute with the arrows. What is new is therefore the compatibility clause `fmap[F] (to i) ∘ arrow₁ ≈ arrow₂`, not initial-object uniqueness as such: `ua_med`/`aua_med` mediators with `_commutes`/`_unique`/`_id`/`_comp`, `universal_arrow_iso`(`_unique`) and `universal_arrow_unique`/`auniversal_arrow_unique`, specialised here as `free_monoid_unique`/`free_monoid_unique_iso` (Awodey Prop 1.10). The (ℕ,+)-on-one-generator consequence is NOT built — issue #802 owns it, cited

(`Instance/Rep.v:176`'s `grp_mon` is not a donor for two reasons: it drops the inverse, and it is `Program`-built with `Qed` obligations, so it would not convert.) The correspondence is a genuine bijection of setoids, **`ns_cong_iso : NS_Setoid ≅[Sets] Cong_Setoid`**, with `congruence_normal_subgroup` as the issue's named alias; `ns_rel N ttt ttt f g = quot_rel N f g` by `eq_refl`, so EVERY #313 lemma about `quot_rel` is a lemma about this congruence, and all four `HomCongruence` fields of `ns_congruence` are #313's `quot_rel_of_equiv`/`_sym`/`_trans`/`_mul` applied verbatim with zero new proof. **Stronger than the exercise asks**: `congruence_iff_normal` holds for an ARBITRARY `Subgroup S` — `HomCongruence (sub_rel S) ↔ S` normal — so the correspondence is with normal subgroups EXACTLY, not merely defined on them. Six strict attempts REFUTED and pinned, the sharpest being that `deloop_quotient N = grp_deloop (QuotientGrp N)` converts on exactly FOUR of the ten fields a `Category` literal supplies (`obj`, `hom`, `id`, `compose`), the other six being built twice. Non-vacuity over #313's S3 (`Instance/Grp/TwoFunctors.v:248` — note there are THREE S3 presentations in tree and `Structure/Groupoid.v:741`'s `S3_Grp` is Deloop's different record): `A3_relates_rotation` merges, `A3_separates_reflection` separates, `S3_refl_sub_no_congruence`. - **Instance/Mod/Quotient.v**, **Instance/Mod/Quotient/Isomorphism.v**, **Instance/Rng/Quotient.v**, **Instance/Rng/Quotient/OneSided.v**: Mac Lane §III.1 Ex 5-6 — quotient modules and quotient rings BY UNIVERSALITY, the module/ring analogue of #313's quotient groups. **The normality layer behaves ASYMMETRICALLY between the two halves, and that asymmetry is the mathematical content.** On the module side there is deliberately NO `NormalSubmodule`: `Record Submodule` carries membership plus FOUR laws, and `smod_normal` — `ns_conj`'s statement transcribed additively, `∀ t a, mem a → mem ((t+a) − t)` — is a THEOREM (`:275`) of an arbitrary submodule. **Commutativity removes TWO fields, not one**: normality, and closure under negation, the latter because the ring's `(−1)` gives `smod_neg = (−1)·a`; the same economy makes `Ideal` five laws with `idl_neg` derived. And the `ns_conj` ↔ two-sidedness correspondence is NOT one-to-one — in `Grp` normality is spent TWICE (`quot_rel_mul` AND `quot_rel_inv`), while in `Rng` two-sidedness is spent ONCE (`rquot_rel_mul` only), additive commutativity handling the negation congruence. **One-sidedness is REFUTED, not assumed**: `Instance/Rng/Quotient/OneSided.v` exhibits `E11Left : LeftIdeal UT2` (the column ℤ·E₁₁ in the upper-triangular 2×2 ring) and `ut2_left_ideal_not_mul_congruence` refutes **the congruence itself** — the property `rquot_rel_mul` establishes and the construction actually consumes — where #313's `S3_refl_sub_not_normal` refutes the closure condition. **Read that comparison precisely — an audit corrected an earlier overclaim here**: the group side ALSO carries a congruence-level refutation at the same subgroup, `Instance/Grp/Congruence.v:1009`'s `S3_refl_sub_no_congruence` (landed by #301), so it is NOT the case that the group side leaves an inference to the reader; what is actually different is only that the ring refutation is DIRECT where the group one goes through `congruence_iff_normal`, and that it lands on the MULTIPLICATION congruence specifically, the clause one-sidedness cannot supply. `ut2_e11_not_right_absorbing` and `E11Left_is_not_an_Ideal` separately refute the dropped closure, and the positive half at the SAME ring is `StrictUpper`, a proper nontrivial two-sided ideal whose quotient does not collapse and **is commutative though UT2 is not**. Both universal elements are over `Theory/Universal/Element.v`'s class with the underlying arrow the projection and the extracted mediator the direct one, both by `eq_refl`. **The three module isomorphism theorems are GRADED, not claimed uniform**, following #313's discipline: the THIRD is a pure universal-property chase (its mediator lemmas destructure nothing; the only element computation is `TmodS`'s saturation obligation, a prerequisite to STATING the theorem); the FIRST derives its comparison from `universal_element_iso` but `mod_image_med` DESTRUCTURES an element, reading the preimage out of the image witness (data, so nothing is chosen), and its uniqueness clause is proved; the SECOND is a corollary composed with `mod_surjective_image_iso` and `mquot_congr`, which ARE hand-built inverse pairs with round trips — degenerate, identity legs, but not instances of `universal_element_iso` — and has no uniqueness clause. **No proof anywhere manipulates a coset**, the setoid quotient forming no coset object, and the header says that is a property of the presentation. **`RModQuotient` is RECONCILED rather than left as a second unshared construction**: `ImageSubmod f : Submodule N`, `rmod_quotient_relations_agree` a biconditional at every pair, and `RModQuotient_is_quotient_by_image : RModQuotient f ≅[RMod R] QuotientMod (ImageSubmod f)` with both legs the identity on elements — the two relations being `{a & x ≈ y + f a}` versus `{a & x − y ≈ f a}`, NOT convertible (pinned as a `Fail` probe), the shuffle between them being the biconditional's whole content. **`AbQuotient` is deliberately NOT unified, and the dependency argument runs OPPOSITE to the one #313 recorded**: `Instance/Ab.v` is strictly UPSTREAM of `Instance/Mod.v` (Mod requires Ab, and `RModQuotient` is literally built on `AbQuotient`), so routing it through a submodule quotient would INVERT an existing dependency — the reason not only still applies but applies with more force, and the honest unifier would be an `AbSubgroup` interface in `Instance/Ab.v` of which `Submodule` is the extension, recorded but not built. `Instance/Ab/Subtract.v` is the shared subtraction calculus, factored so no ring file depends on a module file for it and labelled an upstreaming staging post rather than the first word (`ab_neg_invol` is knowingly its THIRD copy in tree). **A name-collision hazard was found and fixed**: 34 names collided with `Instance/Grp/Quotient/Isomorphism.v`, and since the gate Requires both in one scope, `Print Assumptions first_isomorphism_theorem` would silently have audited the WRONG constant — the module side is renamed `mod_*`/`ImageSubmod`, and zero collisions remain among the four quotient files. Non-vacuity over ℤ throughout (2ℤ proper and nontrivial; 2ℤ ⊃ 4ℤ with the middle quotient proper AND nontrivial for the third theorem; 2ℤ and 3ℤ incomparable with `1 ∈ 2ℤ+3ℤ` for the second) and the ring UMP EXERCISED, the mediator ℤ/6ℤ → ℤ/2ℤ being the class-produced one by `eq_refl`. Deferred: the RING isomorphism theorems (the image of a ring hom is a SUBRING, not an ideal, needing a `Subring` interface the issue does not ask for); no sum/product/intersection of ideals, no principal/prime/maximal ideals, no CRT; no identification of ℤ/2ℤ with `F2_Ring`; no `HasCokernels`; no submodule lattice, correspondence theorem, modular law or exact sequences; no naturality of any isomorphism
- **Instance/Discrete.v**: Discrete categories, and Mac Lane §I.2's "a discrete category is determined by its set of objects" — `DiscreteCat A` (objects `A`, morphisms equality proofs) with `DiscreteCat_Discrete` one way, and the reconstruction the other in Instance/Discrete/Reconstruct.v: `Discrete_iso : Discrete C → DiscreteRigid C → C ≅[StrictCat] DiscreteCat obj[C]`, with the `Cat` reading alongside. `DiscreteRigid` is the extra hypothesis and it is stated INTRINSICALLY — the comparison functor is faithful (`DiscreteRigid_Faithful`) — with UIP on objects and decidable object equality as sufficient conditions (`DiscreteRigid_UIP`, `DiscreteRigid_dec`, the latter by Hedberg, axiom-free). It is not an artifact of the proof: `DiscreteCat`'s homset is strict `eq` on equality proofs, so a functor into it must identify parallel proofs. The file also addresses `Structure/Discrete.v`'s long-standing "Equality is too much here" caveat with the iso-robust `DiscreteUpToIso` (subsingleton homs, all arrows invertible), and then shows the two notions are genuinely independent rather than merely differently phrased: `Discrete_DiscreteUpToIso_forces_UIP` proves that `∀ C, Discrete C → DiscreteUpToIso C` would entail UIP for *every* type, and `Indiscrete_bool_Discrete_absurd` with `Indiscrete_DiscreteUpToIso` exhibits a category that is discrete-up-to-iso but not `Discrete`. Functor categories OVER discrete shapes are Instance/Fun/Discrete.v (Mac Lane §II.4): transformations over a discrete domain are bare families with naturality free (`Discrete_Transform`, with the hom-setoid isomorphism `Discrete_hom_iso : (F ⟹ G) ≅[Sets] (∀ a, F a ~> G a)`), `[DiscreteCat A, B] ≅[Cat] PiCat (fun _ => B)` for ANY index type, the finite power `Fun_Discrete_power : [DiscreteCat (Fin.t n), B] ≅ Pow B n` by Fin-peeling entirely at PiCat level (`Pow` carries a trailing terminal factor as its nullary case — n B-factors ∏ `1` — with the literal `B ∏ B` shape exhibited at n = 2 via `prod_one_r`), the power-set computation `Fun_Discrete_bool_subsets` (functors into `DiscreteCat bool` up to natural iso = characteristic functions `X → bool`, the constructive decidable-subsets reading), the axiom-free Cantor diagonal `cantor_predicates`/`cantor_bool` with `Fun_Discrete_no_surjection` connecting it to the correspondence — the size half of Mac Lane's remark, with the universe-sized half rendered structurally by the library's universe discipline — and Seven Sketches 3.53's morphism half `One_hom_iso` (hom-setoid iso in Sets, any target, instantiated at Sets; the object half `[1, C] ≅ C` is Theory/Shapes.v's `One_Fun_iso`, cited not rebuilt)

- **Instance/Sets/Pointed.v**: `Set_*`, the category of pointed setoids and basepoint-preserving maps (Mac Lane §I.7) — the one-point object is a genuine `ZeroObject` (`zero_coincide := iso_id`); monic ⟺ injective (probed with a two-point object, since the singleton is the zero object and detects nothing) and epi ⟺ surjective (a pointed cokernel-pair gluing `Y + Y` along the image by a closed-form relation — no inductive closure, no decidability, no choice: `pt Y ≈ f (pt X)` is exactly what makes the right leg pointed); Mac Lane's Proposition I.7.2 with its constructive statuses separated — **`pointed_balanced` (invertible ⟺ monic ∧ epi) is unconditional in both directions**, while monic ⇒ split monic needs decidable image membership and epi ⇒ split epi needs decidable equality plus an enumeration (in Set "every epi splits" IS the axiom of choice, so the hypotheses are the honest content, not a formalization gap).
